Entry 1
U: I am feeling hungry so I would like to find a place to eat.
A: Do you have a specific which you want the eating place to be located at?
U: I would like for it to be in San Jose.
A: Is there a specific cuisine type you enjoy, such as Mexican, Italian or something else?
U: I usually like eating the American type of food.
A: I see that at 71 Saint Peter there is a good restaurant which is in San Jose.
U: Can you give me the address of this restaurant.
A: If you want to go to this restaurant you can find it at 71 North San Pedro Street.
U: Can you give me the phone number that I can contact them with?
A: If you want to phone them you can at 408-971-8523.
U: Is there some other restaurant which you can suggest?
A: How would you like Bazille restaurant which is situated in San Jose.
U: Do you have another restaurant matching my needs? For example a restaurant which is economical and is located in Palo Alto.
A: I see that 7 restaurants suit to what you requested. Bird Dog seems as a good restaurant and is located in Palo Alto.
U: Alright, that seems good. I would like to make a booking at this restaurant.
A: For which time do you want the booking to be?
U: I will be eating there at 11:30 am so make it for then.
A: Can you please confirm that you want to book a table for 2 at 11:30 am at the Bird Dog restaurant in Palo Alto for today.
U: That suits me well. Can you tell me if they feature live music?
A: Your booking has been made without errors, but unfortunately they do not have live music.
U: Will I be able to find liquor there? Can you give me the address of their location?
A: The restaurant is located at 420 Ramona Street. Unfortunately they do not serve alcohol at the restaurant.
U: I appreciate it very much. That would be all.
A: Have a good time!
